&+\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}^{\alpha k}\left.\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\beta k}{}^{b}{}_{a}\right|\left.\mathcal T{}_{b}{}^{\beta \gamma}\right|\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}_{\gamma \alpha} -\frac{i}{48}W^{-1}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\left.\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\beta i}{}^{b}{}_{a}\right|\left.\mathcal R(S){}_{b}{}^{\beta}{}_{j}{}^{\alpha j}\right|\\
&-\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}w{}^{\alpha \beta}\left.\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\alpha}{}^{i b c}\right|(\Sigma{}_{b c}){}_{\beta}{}^{\gamma}\big(\nabla_{a}\lambda{}_{\gamma i}\big) -\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}^{\alpha k}\left.\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\beta k}{}^{b c}\right|(\Sigma{}_{b c}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\gamma}\varepsilon{}^{i j}\big(\nabla_{a}w{}^{\beta}{}_{\gamma}\big)\\
&-\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}^{\alpha k}\left.\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\beta k}{}^{b c}\right|(\Sigma{}_{b c}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\gamma}\left.\mathcal T{}_{a}{}^{\beta \delta}\right|\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}_{\delta \gamma} +\frac{i}{48}W^{-1}\lambda{}^{\alpha i}\left.\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\beta i}{}^{b c}\right|(\Sigma{}_{b c}){}_{\alpha \gamma}\left.\mathcal R(S){}_{a}{}^{\beta}{}_{j}{}^{\gamma j}\right|
\end{aligned}
\tag{S4-P-200-152}
